```python
import numpy as np
import time


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        a = a / s
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mx)


def _grad_weights(a, w):
    m = int(a.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    need = 2 * m - 1
    if w.size < need:
        w = np.pad(w, (0, need - w.size), mode="constant")
    elif w.size > need:
        w = w[:need]
    w = np.ascontiguousarray(w)

    if m <= 3000:
        try:
            view = np.lib.stride_tricks.sliding_window_view(w, m)
            if view.shape == (m, m):
                h = view @ a
                if np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
                    return 2.0 * np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            pass
        h = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(m):
            h[j] = float(np.dot(a, w[j:j + m]))
        return 2.0 * h

    N = 1
    while N < 3 * m:
        N <<= 1
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    Wr = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(Ar * np.conj(Wr), N)[:m]
    return 2.0 * np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)


def _make_weights(c, focus, flat_alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    if c.size == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    cm = float(np.max(c))
    idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
    gap = cm - float(c[idx[1]])
    gap = max(gap, 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(cm)))

    beta = float(np.clip(focus / gap, 1.0, 1e6))
    z = np.clip(beta * (c - cm), -60.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        wmax = e / s
    else:
        wmax = np.zeros_like(c)
        wmax[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0

    tol = max(1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(cm)), 0.2 * gap)
    top5 = c[idx[:5]]
    K = int(np.sum(top5 >= cm - tol))
    K = max(1, min(5, K))
    wtop = np.zeros_like(c)
    wtop[idx[:K]] = 1.0 / float(K)

    wcore = 0.6 * wmax + 0.4 * wtop
    alpha = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(flat_alpha)))
    if alpha > 1e-8:
        csum = float(np.sum(c))
        avg = csum / float(c.size) if csum > 0 else 1.0 / float(c.size)
        ex = np.maximum(c - avg, 0.0)
        es = float(np.sum(ex))
        if es > 1e-15:
            wflat = ex / es
        else:
            wflat = wtop
        w = (1.0 - alpha) * wcore + alpha * wflat
    else:
        w = wcore

    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = wtop
    else:
        w = w / s
    return np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)


def _log_from_a(a, limit=25.0):
    a = np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 1e-300)
    y = np.log(a)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y -= float(np.mean(y))
    return np.clip(y, -limit, limit)


def _soft_from_y(y, limit=25.0):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = y - float(np.mean(y))
    y = np.clip(y, -limit, limit)
    z = y - float(np.max(y))
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(y.size, 1.0 / float(y.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return np.asarray(e / s, dtype=np.float64)


def _center_clip(y, limit=25.0):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = y - float(np.mean(y))
    return np.clip(y, -limit, limit)


def _upsample(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return _normalize(a)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    return _normalize(out)


def _make_inits(n, rng, parent=None, max_inits=16):
    n = int(n)
    inits = []

    def add(x):
        try:
            x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if x.size != n or len(inits) >= int(max_inits):
                return
            x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
            x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
            s = float(np.sum(x))
            if s > 1e-12:
                inits.append(x / s)
        except Exception:
            return

    if parent is not None and np.asarray(parent).size > 0:
        p = _upsample(parent, n)
        add(p)
        if rng is not None:
            add(p * np.exp(0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)))
            add(p * np.exp(0.15 * rng.standard_normal(n)))

    add(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n)))

    if n > 1:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        xs = x / float(max(1, n - 1))
        for p in (-1.0, -0.5, -0.2, 0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.6):
            add((x + 1.0) ** p)
        for p in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
            for q in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
                add((x + 1.0) ** p * (float(n) - x) ** q)
        for g in (-3.0, -1.5, -0.5, 0.5, 1.5, 3.0):
            add(np.exp(g * xs))
        for f in (1, 2, 3):
            add(1.0 + 0.15 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * xs) + 1e-6)
        add(1.0 + 0.4 * np.exp(-((xs - 0.2) / 0.1) ** 2) + 0.4 * np.exp(-((xs - 0.8) / 0.1) ** 2))

    if rng is not None:
        add(_soft_from_y(0.5 * rng.standard_normal(n)))
        add(_soft_from_y(1.2 * rng.standard_normal(n)))

    if not inits:
        inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n)))
    return inits


def _lp_direction(f, perturb=0.0, rng=None):
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except Exception:
        return None

    n = int(f.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return _normalize(f)
    if n > 800:
        return None

    ff = np.maximum(np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 1e-12)
    ff = _normalize(ff)

    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n - 1, j] = ff

    cconv = np.convolve(ff, ff)
    M = float(np.max(cconv)) if cconv.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if perturb > 0.0 and rng is not None:
        obj = obj - float(perturb) * rng.random(n)

    b = np.full(2 * n - 1, M * 1.000001, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    try:
        res = linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds)
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is not None and res.success and res.x is not None:
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(g))
        if s > 1e-12:
            return _normalize(g)
    return None


def _lp_blend_once(a, rng=None):
    f = _normalize(a)
    cur = f.copy()
    cur_sc = _score(f)
    best = f.copy()
    best_sc = cur_sc

    g = _lp_direction(f, perturb=(1e-7 if rng is not None else 0.0), rng=rng)
    dirs = []
    if g is not None:
        dirs.append(g)
        dirs.append(g[::-1])

    alphas = (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.55, 0.80)
    for gdir in dirs:
        for alpha in alphas:
            h = (1.0 - alpha) * cur + alpha * gdir
            h = _normalize(h)
            sc = _score(h)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc - 1e-12:
                cur = h.copy()
                cur_sc = sc
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best = h.copy()
                    best_sc = sc
    return best, best_sc


def _lp_alternate(a, rng, max_iter=120, time_limit=60.0):
    f = _normalize(a)
    f_sc = _score(f)
    best = f.copy()
    best_sc = f_sc
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best, best_sc

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.monotonic() > t0 + time_limit:
            break
        g, gsc = _lp_blend_once(f, rng)
        if np.isfinite(gsc) and gsc < f_sc - 1e-12:
            f = g.copy()
            f_sc = gsc
            if f_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = f.copy()
                best_sc = f_sc
            continue

        escape = False
        if rng is not None and time.monotonic() < t0 + time_limit:
            for _e in range(3):
                fp = _normalize(f * np.exp(0.08 * rng.standard_normal(f.size)))
                gp, gpsc = _lp_blend_once(fp, rng)
                if np.isfinite(gpsc) and gpsc < f_sc - 1e-12:
                    f = gp.copy()
                    f_sc = gpsc
                    if f_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best = f.copy()
                        best_sc = f_sc
                    escape = True
                    break
        if not escape:
            break
    return best, best_sc


def _quick_polish(a, rng, max_evals=100, time_limit=5.0):
    best = _normalize(a)
    sc = _score(best)
    n = int(best.size)
    if n <= 1 or max_evals <= 0 or time_limit <= 0:
        return best, sc

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    evals = 0
    while evals < int(max_evals) and time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        if i == j:
            j = (i + 1) % n

        lo = -0.95 * best[i]
        hi = 0.95 * best[j]
        if hi > lo:
            dvals = np.linspace(lo, hi, 7)
            for d in dvals:
                if evals >= int(max_evals):
                    break
                b = best.copy()
                b[i] += float(d)
                b[j] -= float(d)
                if b[i] < -1e-12 or b[j] < -1e-12:
                    continue
                b = np.where(b < 0.0, 0.0, b)
                s = float(np.sum(b))
                if s <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-9:
                    b = b / s
                scb = _score(b)
                evals += 1
                if scb < sc - 1e-12:
                    best = b
                    sc = scb
                    break

        if rng.random() < 0.35:
            L = int(rng.integers(2, max(3, min(n, n // 4 + 2))))
            if L < 2:
                L = 2
            if n <= L:
                L = max(2, n)
            s0 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - L + 1)))
            for f in (0.75, 0.90, 1.10, 1.30, 1.60):
                if evals >= int(max_evals):
                    break
                b = best.copy()
                b[s0:s0 + L] *= float(f)
                s = float(np.sum(b))
                if s <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-9:
                    b = b / s
                scb = _score(b)
                evals += 1
                if scb < sc - 1e-12:
                    best = b
                    sc = scb
                    break
    return best, sc


def _run_optimizer(a0, t_start, t_end, rng,
                   lr_start=0.16, lr_end=0.07,
                   alpha_start=0.60, alpha_end=0.10,
                   focus_start=1.0, focus_end=8.0,
                   cap_start=0.35, cap_end=0.18,
                   polish_every=800, polish_evals=40, polish_time=3.0,
                   max_lp=3):
    if t_end <= time.monotonic() + 0.3:
        return _normalize(a0), _score(a0)

    best = _normalize(a0)
    best_sc = _score(best)
    m = int(best.size)
    y = _log_from_a(best)

    mstate = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    vstate = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    tstate = 0
    mb1 = 0.0
    mb2 = 0.0
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    capv = cap_start
    lp_calls = 0
    span = max(1e-6, float(t_end - t_start))

    while time.monotonic() < t_end - 0.3:
        it += 1
        frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, (time.monotonic() - t_start) / span))
        alpha = alpha_start + (alpha_end - alpha_start) * frac
        focus = focus_start + (focus_end - focus_start) * frac
        lr = lr_start + (lr_end - lr_start) * frac
        target_cap = cap_start + (cap_end - cap_start) * frac

        y = _center_clip(y)
        a = _soft_from_y(y)
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        cm = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cm):
            break
        sc = 2.0 * m * cm

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            capv = target_cap
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            capv = max(0.05, min(target_cap, capv * 0.985))

        w = _make_weights(c, focus, alpha)
        ga = _grad_weights(a, w)
        gz = a * (ga - float(np.dot(ga, a)))
        gz = np.where(np.isfinite(gz), gz, 0.0)
        if float(np.linalg.norm(gz)) < 1e-14:
            gz = 1e-3 * rng.standard_normal(m)

        tstate += 1
        mb1 = (1.0 - b1) + b1 * mb1
        mb2 = (1.0 - b2) + b2 * mb2
        mstate = b1 * mstate + (1.0 - b1) * gz
        vstate = b2 * vstate + (1.0 - b2) * (gz * gz)
        step = lr * (mstate / mb1) / (np.sqrt(vstate / mb2) + eps)
        step = np.clip(step, -capv, capv)
        y = y - step

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
            y = _log_from_a(best) + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(m)
            mstate.fill(0.0)
            vstate.fill(0.0)
            tstate = 0
            mb1 = 0.0
            mb2 = 0.0
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        if polish_every > 0 and it % int(polish_every) == 0 and t_end - time.monotonic() > polish_time + 1.0:
            pol, polsc = _quick_polish(best, rng, max_evals=polish_evals, time_limit=polish_time)
            if np.isfinite(polsc) and polsc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = pol
                best_sc = polsc
                y = _log_from_a(best) + 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(m)
                mstate.fill(0.0)
                vstate.fill(0.0)
                tstate = 0
                mb1 = 0.0
                mb2 = 0.0
                no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 600 or (no_improve > 200 and sc > best_sc + 0.02):
            did = False
            if lp_calls < int(max_lp) and t_end - time.monotonic() > 8.0 and m <= 800:
                lp_calls += 1
                pol, polsc = _lp_blend_once(best, rng)
                if np.isfinite(polsc) and polsc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best = pol
                    best_sc = polsc
                    did = True
            if not did:
                if time.monotonic() > t_end - 3.0:
                    break
                noise = 0.04 + 0.06 * min(1.0, float(it) / 5000.0)
                y = _log_from_a(best) + noise * rng.standard_normal(m)
                mstate.fill(0.0)
                vstate.fill(0.0)
                tstate = 0
                mb1 = 0.0
                mb2 = 0.0
                no_improve = 0

    return best, best_sc


def _lbfgs_refine(a, beta=8000.0, maxiter=60, maxfun=150):
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import minimize
    except Exception:
        a = _normalize(a)
        return a, _score(a)

    a0 = _normalize(a)
    m = int(a0.size)
    if m < 2:
        return a0, _score(a0)

    y0 = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300))
    y0 = y0 - float(y0[-1])
    d0 = y0[:m - 1].copy()

    def fun_grad(d):
        d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        y = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
        y[:m - 1] = d
        y[m - 1] = 0.0
        z = y - float(np.max(y))
        e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0))
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return 1e100, np.zeros(m - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        aa = e / s

        c = np.convolve(aa, aa)
        if c.size == 0:
            return 1e100, np.zeros(m - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        cm = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cm):
            return 1e100, np.zeros(m - 1, dtype=np.float64)

        if beta <= 0:
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            logsum = cm
        else:
            zc = np.clip(float(beta) * (c - cm), -60.0, 0.0)
            e2 = np.exp(zc)
            s2 = float(np.sum(e2))
            if s2 > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s2):
                w = e2 / s2
            else:
                w = np.zeros_like(c)
                w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            logsum = cm + float(np.log(max(s2, 1e-300))) / float(beta)

        phi = float(2.0 * m * logsum)
        if not np.isfinite(phi):
            return 1e100, np.zeros(m - 1, dtype=np.float64)

        ga = _grad_weights(aa, (2.0 * m) * w)
        gz = aa * (ga - float(np.dot(ga, aa)))
        gz = np.where(np.isfinite(gz), gz, 0.0)
        return phi, gz[:m - 1]

    try:
        res = minimize(fun_grad, d0, jac=True, method="L-BFGS-B",
                       options={
                           "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                           "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                           "ftol": 1e-12,
                           "gtol": 1e-8,
                           "disp": False
                       })
    except Exception:
        return a0, _score(a0)

    d = d0
    if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
        dx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if dx.size == m - 1 and np.all(np.isfinite(dx)):
            d = dx

    y = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
    y[:m - 1] = d
    y[m - 1] = 0.0
    aa = _soft_from_y(y)
    sc = _score(aa)
    ref = _score(a0)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < ref - 1e-12:
        return aa, sc
    return a0, ref


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    tb = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    headroom = min(25.0, max(1.0, 0.02 * max(10.0, tb)))
    if headroom >= tb:
        headroom = max(0.1, 0.1 * tb)
    hard = t0 + tb - headroom

    parent = None
    n_main = 127
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0:
            pp = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            pp = np.maximum(pp, 0.0)
            s = float(np.sum(pp))
            if s > 0.01:
                parent = pp / s
                n_main = int(parent.size)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_main)

    if parent.size > 2048:
        parent = _upsample(parent, 2048)
        n_main = 2048

    best = parent.copy()
    best_sc = _score(best)

    for a in _make_inits(n_main, rng, parent=parent, max_inits=14):
        sc = _score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy()
            best_sc = sc

    if hard - time.monotonic() < 15.0:
        final_list = [float(x) for x in best]
        try:
            evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        except Exception:
            final_list = [1.0]
        return final_list

    remaining = hard - time.monotonic()
    hetero_budget = 0.0
    if remaining > 400.0:
        hetero_budget = min(100.0, 0.10 * remaining)
    main_end = hard - hetero_budget - 2.0

    lp_end = min(main_end, time.monotonic() + min(120.0, 0.25 * max(0.0, main_end - time.monotonic())))
    if lp_end > time.monotonic() + 12.0:
        best, best_sc = _lp_alternate(best, rng, max_iter=120, time_limit=max(0.0, lp_end - time.monotonic()))
        # If LP helped, immediate polish.
        best, best_sc = _quick_polish(best, rng, max_evals=120, time_limit=min(8.0, max(1.0, main_end - time.monotonic() - 0.5)))

    now = time.monotonic()
    end1 = min(main_end, now + 0.5 * max(0.0, main_end - now))
    if end1 > now + 5.0:
        best, best_sc = _run_optimizer(
            best, now, end1, rng,
            lr_start=0.16, lr_end=0.09,
            alpha_start=0.55, alpha_end=0.20,
            focus_start=1.0, focus_end=6.0,
            cap_start=0.35, cap_end=0.20,
            polish_every=900, polish_evals=40, polish_time=3.0, max_lp=2
        )
        now = time.monotonic()

    if main_end > now + 5.0:
        best, best_sc = _run_optimizer(
            best, now, main_end, rng,
            lr_start=0.09, lr_end=0.04,
            alpha_start=0.12, alpha_end=0.0,
            focus_start=5.0, focus_end=12.0,
            cap_start=0.22, cap_end=0.12,
            polish_every=800, polish_evals=50, polish_time=4.0, max_lp=4
        )

    if main_end > time.monotonic() + 6.0 and best.size <= 512:
        for beta in (800.0, 8000.0, 80000.0):
            if time.monotonic() > main_end - 4.0:
                break
            b2, b2sc = _lbfgs_refine(best, beta=beta, maxiter=60, maxfun=150)
            if np.isfinite(b2sc) and b2sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = b2
                best_sc = b2sc

    if main_end > time.monotonic() and best.size <= 800:
        for _ in range(2):
            b2, b2sc = _lp_blend_once(best, rng)
            if np.isfinite(b2sc) and b2sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = b2
                best_sc = b2sc
            else:
                break

    polish_time_left = hard - time.monotonic() - 0.5
    if polish_time_left > 2.0:
        best, best_sc = _quick_polish(best, rng, max_evals=500, time_limit=max(5.0, min(35.0, polish_time_left)))

    if hetero_budget > 15.0 and time.monotonic() < hard - 12.0:
        hetero_end = hard - 8.0
        lengths = []
        for L in (
            n_main - 2, n_main - 1, n_main + 1, n_main + 2,
            int(round(n_main * 1.15)), int(round(n_main * 1.30)), int(round(n_main * 1.50))
        ):
            if 2 <= L <= 768 and L != n_main:
                lengths.append(int(L))
        lengths = sorted(set(lengths))

        if lengths:
            per_time = max(5.0, min(20.0, (hetero_end - time.monotonic()) / float(len(lengths))))
            for L in lengths:
                if time.monotonic() > hetero_end - 5.0:
                    break
                arr = _upsample(best, L)
                sc = _score(arr)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best = arr.copy()
                    best_sc = sc
                for a in _make_inits(L, rng, parent=best, max_inits=6):
                    sc = _score(a)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best = a.copy()
                        best_sc = sc
                cand = _upsample(best, L)
                cand_sc = _score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(cand_sc) and cand_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_sc = cand_sc

                endL = min(hetero_end - 2.0, time.monotonic() + per_time)
                if endL > time.monotonic() + 3.0 and cand.size <= 768:
                    cand2, sc2 = _run_optimizer(
                        cand, time.monotonic(), endL, rng,
                        lr_start=0.12, lr_end=0.05,
                        alpha_start=0.40, alpha_end=0.0,
                        focus_start=3.0, focus_end=10.0,
                        cap_start=0.25, cap_end=0.12,
                        polish_every=600, polish_evals=30, polish_time=2.0, max_lp=2
                    )
                    cand_p, scp = _quick_polish(cand2, rng, max_evals=80, time_limit=3.0)
                    if np.isfinite(scp) and scp <= sc2:
                        cand2 = cand_p
                        sc2 = scp
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best = cand2.copy()
                        best_sc = sc2

    candidates = []
    if parent is not None:
        candidates.append(parent)
    if best is not None:
        candidates.append(best)
    candidates.append(np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64))

    final_list = None
    final_score = np.inf
    for cand in candidates:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size == 0:
                continue
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
            if float(np.sum(arr)) < 0.01:
                continue
            lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
                final_score = sc
                final_list = lst
        except Exception:
            continue

    if final_list is None:
        final_list = [1.0]
    return final_list
```
